Now we need to compute turns needed for troops based on speed.

Speed factor: slow=1 edge per turn, medium=1.5 edges per turn, fast=2 edges per turn. Turns = ceil(distance / speed_factor).

We'll need to decide which troop types we send; the army speed is slowest type.

Thus if we send any spearman (slow), speed = slow => turns = distance (since slow factor 1). If we send only archers and/or knights (no spearman), speed = medium if any archer present? Actually slowest among present types: if we have archers (medium) and knights (fast), slowest is medium => speed = medium. If we have only knights (fast), speed = fast.

Thus we can compute travel time.
